183507 DIGGING DOWN THROUGH THE CENTURIES: U. OF PENNA. EXPEDITION TO BEISAN

The Gibraltar in which the successive tides of invasion between Egypts and the East broke century after century has recently been laid bare of its archaeological records by an expedition sent out by the University of Pennsylvania Museum.
This photo shows the tents of the members of the expedition. This is the spot where the Philistines camped, with Mount Gilboa and the battlefield where Saul met his death at the hands of the Philistines, in the background. Beisan is ten miles from Nazareth, a place on which more battles have been fought than on any other similarly sized spot in the history of the world.
